TARGET - Theorem A: fixed-base linear causal depth and sharpness for Pisot numeration.

Let U = (u_j) be a strictly increasing Pisot numeration system, non-standard initial values
allowed, u_0 = 1, with canonical greedy digit set D_U = {0,...,d_U}. Let X_{U,m} be the
canonical greedy U-normal words of length m, i.e. x in D_U^m with sum_{j<=k} x_j u_j <
u_{k+1} for 0 <= k < m, so Val_{U,m} bijects X_{U,m} onto {0,...,u_m - 1}. Define
Fold_{U,m}(w) = (Val_{U,m}|_{X_{U,m}})^{-1}(Val_{U,m}(w) mod u_m) and the sliding code
Phi_{U,m}. Assume U preserves zeros, i.e. its leading Pisot root satisfies Condition F.

Prove:

 (A1) There is a constant C_U < infinity, EFFECTIVELY COMPUTABLE from the recurrence, the
      canonical digit set and the bounded-zero normalization automaton, such that for every
      m >= 2, Phi_{U,m} injective implies l_cau(U,m) <= C_U m. Equivalently, in the
      corresponding difference-collision graph, if no first-coordinate-nonzero state can
      reach a directed cycle, every directed path from that initial state set has length at
      most C_U m - 1.

 (A2) For theta the real root of x^3 - 2x^2 + x - 1 and U_theta = (Q_j(theta))_j, for all
      m >= 4: Phi_{theta,m} is injective and l_cau(theta,m) = 2*floor(m/2) - 1.

 (A3) With lambda_m = 2*floor(m/2) - 1, prove N_{theta,m,lambda_m - 1} nonempty and
      N_{theta,m,lambda_m} empty, and preferably classify the unique terminal collision pair
      N_{theta,m,lambda_m - 1} = {±E_m}.

Hence lim_{m->infinity} l_cau(theta,m)/m = 1, so the linear upper bound under a fixed Pisot
numeration cannot be improved to o(m) in growth order.

Take the exact statement, notation and constants from HIS report, not from this paraphrase.

WHY IT RAISES THE TIER, AND WHAT YOU MUST THEREFORE NOT COMPROMISE. He gives three reasons
and each is a constraint on the work:
 1. It lifts the object of study from parameter families of a self-defined beta-rank-fold to
    a UNIFORM COMPLEXITY THEOREM OVER STANDARD PISOT NUMERATION SYSTEMS. If the theorem ends
    up quantified only over our own constructed family, it has failed.
 2. It compresses the current general bound l_cau <= (2d+1)^{m-1} - 1, which is exponential
    and comes from counting nonzero difference-graph vertices without using Pisot
    contraction or the fine structure of the normalization automaton, down to a LINEAR bound
    for a fixed system. C_U must genuinely come from that fine structure, not from a
    re-derived vertex count.
 3. The fixed-theta formula then becomes a MATCHING LOWER BOUND rather than an isolated
    pretty example. (A2) and (A3) are what make (A1) sharp; do not deliver (A1) alone and
    call the target met.

HONEST OUTCOMES: at 35 percent, failure is likely and acceptable. If (A1) does not close,
say so in the FIRST LINE of your report and give the exact step that fails - in particular
whether the obstruction is in making C_U effective, or in the reachability argument for

if (A1) does not. Do not quietly restrict the class of Pisot numeration systems until (A1)
becomes true but covers only our own family; that is the specific failure mode that would

## Instruction Priority

Superpowers skills override default system prompt behavior, but **user instructions always take precedence**:

1. **User's explicit instructions** (CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, AGENTS.md, direct requests) â€” highest priority
2. **Superpowers skills** â€” override default system behavior where they conflict
3. **Default system prompt** â€” lowest priority

If CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, or AGENTS.md says "don't use TDD" and a skill says "always use TDD," follow the user's instructions. The user is in control.

## How to Access Skills

**In Claude Code:** Use the `Skill` tool. When you invoke a skill, its content is loaded and presented to youâ€”follow it directly. Never use the Read tool on skill files.

**In Copilot CLI:** Use the `skill` tool. Skills are auto-discovered from installed plugins. The `skill` tool works the same as Claude Code's `Skill` tool.

**In Gemini CLI:** Skills activate via the `activate_skill` tool. Gemini loads skill metadata at session start and activates the full content on demand.

**In other environments:** Check your platform's documentation for how skills are loaded.

## Platform Adaptation

Skills use Claude Code tool names. Non-CC platforms: see `references/copilot-tools.md` (Copilot CLI), `references/codex-tools.md` (Codex) for tool equivalents. Gemini CLI users get the tool mapping loaded automatically via GEMINI.md.

# Using Skills

## The Rule

**Invoke relevant or requested skills BEFORE any response or action.** Even a 1% chance a skill might apply means that you should invoke the skill to check. If an invoked skill turns out to be wrong for the situation, you don't need to use it.

```dot
digraph skill_flow {
    "User message received" [shape=doublecircle];
    "About to EnterPlanMode?" [shape=doublecircle];
    "Already brainstormed?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" [shape=box];
    "Might any skill apply?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke Skill tool" [shape=box];
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" [shape=box];
    "Has checklist?" [shape=diamond];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [shape=box];
    "Follow skill exactly" [shape=box];
    "Respond (including clarifications)" [shape=doublecircle];

    "About to EnterPlanMode?" -> "Already brainstormed?";
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Invoke brainstorming skill" [label="no"];
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Might any skill apply?" [label="yes"];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" -> "Might any skill apply?";

    "User message received" -> "Might any skill apply?";
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Invoke Skill tool" [label="yes, even 1%"];
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Respond (including clarifications)" [label="definitely not"];
    "Invoke Skill tool" -> "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'";
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" -> "Has checklist?";
    "Has checklist?" -> "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [label="yes"];
    "Has checklist?" -> "Follow skill exactly" [label="no"];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" -> "Follow skill exactly";
}
```

## Red Flags

These thoughts mean STOPâ€”you're rationalizing:

| Thought | Reality |
|---------|---------|
| "This is just a simple question" | Questions are tasks. Check for skills. |
| "I need more context first" | Skill check comes BEFORE clarifying questions. |
| "Let me explore the codebase first" | Skills tell you HOW to explore. Check first. |
| "I can check git/files quickly" | Files lack conversation context. Check for skills. |
| "Let me gather information first" | Skills tell you HOW to gather information. |
| "This doesn't need a formal skill" | If a skill exists, use it. |
| "I remember this skill" | Skills evolve. Read current version. |
| "This doesn't count as a task" | Action = task. Check for skills. |
| "The skill is overkill" | Simple things become complex. Use it. |
| "I'll just do this one thing first" | Check BEFORE doing anything. |
| "This feels productive" | Undisciplined action wastes time. Skills prevent this. |
| "I know what that means" | Knowing the concept â‰  using the skill. Invoke it. |

## Skill Priority

When multiple skills could apply, use this order:

1. **Process skills first** (brainstorming, debugging) - these determine HOW to approach the task
2. **Implementation skills second** (frontend-design, mcp-builder) - these guide execution

"Let's build X" â†’ brainstorming first, then implementation skills.
"Fix this bug" â†’ debugging first, then domain-specific skills.

## Skill Types

**Rigid** (TDD, debugging): Follow exactly. Don't adapt away discipline.

**Flexible** (patterns): Adapt principles to context.

The skill itself tells you which.

## User Instructions

Instructions say WHAT, not HOW. "Add X" or "Fix Y" doesn't mean skip workflows.

name: writing-plans
description: Use when you have a spec or requirements for a multi-step task, before touching code
---

# Writing Plans

## Overview

Write comprehensive implementation plans assuming the engineer has zero context for our codebase and questionable taste. Document everything they need to know: which files to touch for each task, code, testing, docs they might need to check, how to test it. Give them the whole plan as bite-sized tasks. DRY. YAGNI. TDD. Frequent commits.

Assume they are a skilled developer, but know almost nothing about our toolset or problem domain. Assume they don't know good test design very well.

**Announce at start:** "I'm using the writing-plans skill to create the implementation plan."

**Context:** This should be run in a dedicated worktree (created by brainstorming skill).

**Save plans to:** `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<feature-name>.md`
- (User preferences for plan location override this default)

## Scope Check

If the spec covers multiple independent subsystems, it should have been broken into sub-project specs during brainstorming. If it wasn't, suggest breaking this into separate plans — one per subsystem. Each plan should produce working, testable software on its own.

## File Structure

Before defining tasks, map out which files will be created or modified and what each one is responsible for. This is where decomposition decisions get locked in.

- Design units with clear boundaries and well-defined interfaces. Each file should have one clear responsibility.
- You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. Prefer smaller, focused files over large ones that do too much.
- Files that change together should live together. Split by responsibility, not by technical layer.
- In existing codebases, follow established patterns. If the codebase uses large files, don't unilaterally restructure - but if a file you're modifying has grown unwieldy, including a split in the plan is reasonable.

This structure informs the task decomposition. Each task should produce self-contained changes that make sense independently.

## Bite-Sized Task Granularity

**Each step is one action (2-5 minutes):**
- "Write the failing test" - step
- "Run it to make sure it fails" - step
- "Implement the minimal code to make the test pass" - step
- "Run the tests and make sure they pass" - step
- "Commit" - step

## Plan Document Header

**Every plan MUST start with this header:**

```markdown
# [Feature Name] Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** [One sentence describing what this builds]

**Architecture:** [2-3 sentences about approach]

**Tech Stack:** [Key technologies/libraries]

---
```

## Task Structure

````markdown
### Task N: [Component Name]

**Files:**
- Create: `exact/path/to/file.py`
- Modify: `exact/path/to/existing.py:123-145`
- Test: `tests/exact/path/to/test.py`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```python
def test_specific_behavior():
    result = function(input)
    assert result == expected
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**

Run: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`
Expected: FAIL with "function not defined"

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

```python
def function(input):
    return expected
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**

Run: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`
Expected: PASS

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add tests/path/test.py src/path/file.py
git commit -m "feat: add specific feature"
```
````

## No Placeholders

Every step must contain the actual content an engineer needs. These are **plan failures** — never write them:
- "TBD", "TODO", "implement later", "fill in details"
- "Add appropriate error handling" / "add validation" / "handle edge cases"
- "Write tests for the above" (without actual test code)
- "Similar to Task N" (repeat the code — the engineer may be reading tasks out of order)
- Steps that describe what to do without showing how (code blocks required for code steps)
- References to types, functions, or methods not defined in any task

## Remember
- Exact file paths always
- Complete code in every step — if a step changes code, show the code
- Exact commands with expected output
- DRY, YAGNI, TDD, frequent commits

## Self-Review

After writing the complete plan, look at the spec with fresh eyes and check the plan against it. This is a checklist you run yourself — not a subagent dispatch.

**1. Spec coverage:** Skim each section/requirement in the spec. Can you point to a task that implements it? List any gaps.

**2. Placeholder scan:** Search your plan for red flags — any of the patterns from the "No Placeholders" section above. Fix them.

**3. Type consistency:** Do the types, method signatures, and property names you used in later tasks match what you defined in earlier tasks? A function called `clearLayers()` in Task 3 but `clearFullLayers()` in Task 7 is a bug.

If you find issues, fix them inline. No need to re-review — just fix and move on. If you find a spec requirement with no task, add the task.

## Execution Handoff

After saving the plan, offer execution choice:

**"Plan complete and saved to `docs/superpowers/plans/<filename>.md`. Two execution options:**

**1. Subagent-Driven (recommended)** - I dispatch a fresh subagent per task, review between tasks, fast iteration

**2. Inline Execution** - Execute tasks in this session using executing-plans, batch execution with checkpoints

**Which approach?"**

**If Subagent-Driven chosen:**
- **REQUIRED SUB-SKILL:** Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development
- Fresh subagent per task + two-stage review

**If Inline Execution chosen:**
- **REQUIRED SUB-SKILL:** Use superpowers:executing-plans
- Batch execution with checkpoints for review

---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

Claiming work is complete without verification is dishonesty, not efficiency.

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## Why This Matters

From 24 failure memories:
- your human partner said "I don't believe you" - trust broken
- Undefined functions shipped - would crash
- Missing requirements shipped - incomplete features
- Time wasted on false completion → redirect → rework
- Violates: "Honesty is a core value. If you lie, you'll be replaced."

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

## The Bottom Line

**No shortcuts for verification.**

Run the command. Read the output. THEN claim the result.

This is non-negotiable.

总裁决
严格结论：按现有机器，只增加“固定 θ 的精确深度公式”仍不足以使论文上升一个完整层级。
它会显著加强论文，因为它同时固定了代数次数、基数、Parry 语言和二进制字母表，排除了 Bassino 族中“无界性只是参数和字母表一起增长”的解释；但它仍然是对本文自定义 cyclic rank-fold 的另一个精确模型。较高层级审稿人仍可用一句话压住它：

这是一个精巧构造族的精确分析，而不是关于 Pisot numeration、β-normalization 或 β-dynamics 的结构定理。

现稿自己已经非常清楚地区分了整数语言秩同余与数值 β-normalization，且指出两者没有被识别。 现有 Fischer 状态数也明确依赖固定输出字母表，而非共轭不变量。
因此：

若“可达”要求成功概率至少约 1/2，则诚实答案是：当前不存在一个同时可达且足以升一层的单一结果；按现有机器，不能升。

但有一个边缘可达、且一旦成功我会承认它足以升层的精确定理。它应成为唯一的升层目标。

1. 单一最有价值的新结果
首选：固定基数线性因果深度与 sharpness 定理
建议把目标写成下面这个完整定理，而不是只写固定 θ 的公式。
定理 A：Pisot numeration 的固定基数线性深度定理
设 U=(uj​)j≥0​ 是一个严格递增的 Pisot numeration system，允许非标准初值，u0​=1，其 canonical greedy digit set 为有限集
DU​={0,…,dU​}.
设 XU,m​⊂DUm​ 是长度 m 的 canonical greedy U-正规词集；等价地，
x=(x0​,…,xm−1​)∈XU,m​⟺j=0∑k​xj​uj​<uk+1​(0≤k<m).
于是
ValU,m​(x)=j=0∑m−1​xj​uj​
把 XU,m​ 双射到 {0,…,um​−1}。定义
FoldU,m​(w)=(ValU,m​∣XU,m​​)−1(ValU,m​(w)modum​)
及相应滑动码 ΦU,m​。
假设 U preserves zeros，亦即其 leading Pisot root 满足 Condition F。则存在一个可由 U 的递推、canonical digit set 和 bounded-zero normalization automaton 有效计算的常数
CU​<∞
使得，对每个 m≥2，
ΦU,m​ 单射⟹ℓcau​(U,m)≤CU​m.(A1)
等价地，在相应差分碰撞图中，若首坐标非零状态不可达有向环，则所有从该初态集出发的有向路径长度均至多 CU​m−1。
此外，令 θ 为
x3−2x2+x−1
的实根，并令 Uθ=(Qj​(θ))j≥0​。则对所有 m≥4，
Φθ,m​ 单射,ℓcau​(θ,m)=2⌊2m​⌋−1.(A2)
更强地，若
λm​=2⌊2m​⌋−1,
则应证明
Nθ,m,λm​−1​=∅,Nθ,m,λm​​=∅,(A3)
并最好进一步分类出
Nθ,m,λm​−1​={±Em​}
的唯一终端碰撞对。
由此
m→∞lim​mℓcau​(θ,m)​=1,
所以固定 Pisot numeration 下的线性上界在增长阶上不能改进为 o(m)。

为什么这个定理能升层
它完成了三件固定 θ 公式单独做不到的事。
第一，它把研究对象从“某个自定义 β-rank-fold 的若干参数族”提升为标准 Pisot numeration system 上的统一复杂度定理。
第二，它把现稿一般界
ℓcau​≤(2d+1)m−1−1
从状态枚举给出的指数界，压到固定系统下的线性界。现稿的指数界来自差分图非零顶点数，本身没有使用 Pisot 收缩或 normalization automaton 的细结构。
第三，固定 θ 的公式成为 matching lower bound，而不再是孤立的漂亮例子。这样论文的中心会从：

“我们精确算出了几个 arithmetic local codes”

转变为：

“固定 Pisot numeration 的 cyclic normalization 深度至多线性，而且线性阶在一个固定二进制三次系统上精确达到。”

这是结构性结论，而不是再增加一个族。

上一轮固定 θ 候选的裁决
若只证明
∀m≥4,Φθ,m​ 单射,ℓcau​(θ,m)=2⌊m/2⌋−1,(Θ)
我的判断是：
数学上有价值，优先权明显增强，但仍不足以稳定升一个完整层级。
它确实排除了：


字母表随 n 增长；


基数随 n 增长；


Parry word 长度随 n 增长；


Bassino 参数中大首位数字带来的强分离。


所以它比现稿的 Bassino 定理有真正不同的含义。现稿的 Bassino 结果依靠参数一致的两窗口 carry exclusion 和精确终端路径归纳。  但固定 θ 仍然只说明本文映射的一种新现象，未给出全类上界，也没有把该深度变成标准 numeration invariant。
我的层级影响评分只有约 0.45/1：足以让现稿更强，不足以单独改变审稿人的根本分类。

次选：完全闭合二次负共轭室
精确定理应为：
定理 B：负共轭二次 Pisot 的统一两输出解码
令 β 为
x2−ax−b,a≥b≥1
的 Pisot 根。则对每个 m≥3，
ℓcau​(β,m)=2.(B)
这会把二次结论完整化为
ℓcau​(β,m)=⎩⎨⎧​2,3,​β′<0, m≥3,β′>0, m≥3.​
但它只是关闭现稿已经明确列出的接口。现稿也准确指出，所缺的是对全部 bounded-carry branches 的穷尽不变量，而不能只按 Qm​ 的倍数和商的符号分类。 因而它是很好的 completion theorem，却不是升层定理。

2. 各候选能否由现有 machinery 达到
2.1 定理 A：统一线性上界加固定 θ sharpness
现有机器中可直接输入的部分
现稿已经给出最重要的第一步：
ℓcau​(β,m)=min{L:Nβ,m,L​=∅},
并把它等价成差分图中从首坐标非零状态出发的最长生存路径。
还已有：


差分状态商保留全部碰撞，而不是只给必要条件；


零状态唯一前驱；


单射、有限 future-only inverse、不可达有向环三者等价；


有向环给出周期碰撞证书；


Bassino carry exclusion；


Bassino terminal-path induction；


quadratic recurrence annihilator 与 nearest-multiple separation。


现稿已经把 classical pair graph 与新的 arithmetic quotient 分开，这一定位现在是正确的。
真正可用的标准对象桥
设一个碰撞窗口满足
j=0∑m−1​uj​et+j​=kt​um​.
则它等价于一个标准 U-零表示：
[et​,et+1​,…,et+m−1​,−kt​]U​=0,(2.1)
其中最后一个坐标位于权 um​。
对固定 U，由于 uj​≍βj，
∣kt​∣≤dU​um​∑j<m​uj​​
有一个与 m 无关的统一上界。因此所有窗口都属于某个固定有限字母表上的 bounded zero-representation language。
这是真正的非循环桥。Pisot numeration 的 bounded zero representations 构成正则语言，是标准 normalization 理论中的结果。arXiv 2026 年的新工作又把这类零表示、normalization 和 zero-preservation 常数 Kc​ 放入标准 U-adic 框架。arXiv
真正缺少的新 ingredient
缺少的不是“再画一张 pair graph”，而是：

重叠零表示条带的线性压缩/泵引理。

具体必须证明：
给定固定的 bounded-zero automaton，若长度 m+1 的 accepted zero words 按一个坐标逐次重叠，且相应差分路径不存在可泵成双边周期碰撞的循环，则该重叠链长度至多 CU​m。
标准正则语言 pumping lemma 本身不够。宽度为 m 的重叠积可以有指数多个 strip states；如果不使用 Pisot recurrence 的边界消去、共轭收缩或 carry 单调性，仍然只能得到指数界。
所需新引理应同时使用：


固定阶递推消去窗口内部；


kt​ 的固定有限 carry alphabet；


两端边界状态，而非整个 m−1 位差分词；


Pisot 共轭方向的收缩；


“重复相同边界类型会泵出可达周期碰撞”的严格结论。


这是一个真实的新证明部件，而不是把目标定理改名为“线性路径引理”。
可达性判断
边缘可达，成功概率约 0.35。
风险在于一般固定 Pisot recurrence 的重叠条带可能仍具有比线性更长的无环暂态；正则性不会自动排除这一点。要么找到上述两端压缩，要么就可能发现定理 A 本身为假。
若它为假，而能构造一个固定 Pisot numeration 上超线性、甚至指数级的 ℓcau​，那反例本身也会是较高层级结果；但现稿目前没有任何数据支持这种方向。

2.2 固定 θ 精确公式
对
θ3−2θ2+θ−1=0
有
dθ​(1)=11010∞,
且
Q0​=1,Q1​=2,Q2​=4,Qj+3​=2Qj+2​−Qj+1​+Qj​.
现有机器可以输入：


Theorem 6.2 的 Toeplitz obstruction；


zero-predecessor lemma；


Bassino terminal induction 的组织方式；


cubic conjugate energy estimate 的思路；


有限图中提示的唯一终端路径及奇偶配对。


真正缺的是：
两步 aperture renormalization
需要证明一个 m↦m−2 的精确递归：


所有非终止 carry branches 都被排除；


唯一最长路径在去掉两端固定短块后化为 aperture m−2 的唯一最长路径；


奇数 m=2r+1 与前一个偶数 2r 具有同一非零核心，只多一个终端零；


终端 obstruction 只有 ±Em​。


现稿 Bassino 的 Lemma 6.7 不能直接移植。那里关键使用
Qj+1​>nQj​
以及随参数 n 增大的强分离；固定 θ 的比例不到 2，而其最小递推还含负系数。现稿的 quadratic nearest-multiple separation 也不能替代这一工作。
因此上一轮的 0.82 成功率偏高。我的复评是：
0.70​
它仍是当前最可做的内部加强，但参数一致的“无旁支”证明是实质困难，不是有限枚举后补一个归纳句即可。

2.3 负共轭二次室的 ℓcau​=2
现有输入非常充分：


Lemma 5.3 nearest-multiple separation；


Lemma 5.4 bounded sliding congruences；


二次递推的二阶消去；


Pa,b−​ 的短 bounded multiple (b,a,−1)；


Theorem 6.2 的最长路径判据。


真正缺少的仅是现稿自己指出的：

对两个连续同余的全部 carry types 给出闭合且参数一致的有限状态不变量。

这里不需要引进 natural extensions、tiles 或外部数论。成功概率很高：
0.88​
但即使成功，仍只是完成二次表格；层级影响有限。

3. “成功概率 × 层级影响”排序
这里“层级影响”取 0 到 1：1 表示在证明和写作都合格时，足以实质改变论文的优先级判断；不是对定理真假的概率。
排名候选成功概率层级影响乘积裁决1定理 A：固定 Pisot numeration 下 O(m) 上界，加固定 θ 的 Θ(m) sharpness0.350.950.333唯一真正升层候选2固定 θ：ℓcau​=2⌊m/2⌋−1 及唯一终端路径0.700.450.315最佳内部加强，但单独不升层3全部负共轭二次基数、全部 m≥3：ℓcau​=20.880.180.158应闭合，但只是 completion4标准 Fibonacci numeration 的最优 zero-preservation 常数 K2​0.550.250.138标准对象，但体量太小，宜另文5把 rank-fold 深度直接识别为 U-adic 群上平移或环面编码的标准 delay0.080.980.078影响极高，但当前桥很可能不存在6Hejda–Steiner 的 quadratic β-adic prefix 问题0.021.000.020当前机器到不了
第四项值得附带说明。2026 年 8 月的 U-adic 预印本定义了 Kc​，并称计算提示 Fibonacci 情形 K2​=3。arXiv 但按其定义，取标准 Fibonacci 权
u0​=u1​=1, u2​=2,…
及
g5​=−2,g6​=2,g7​=−2,g9​=−2
而其余坐标为零，则
[g]U​=−2u5​+2u6​−2u7​−2u9​=−142.
另一方面
142=u10​+u8​+u6​+u4​+u1​=89+34+13+5+1,
故
ord(g)=5,νU​(g)=1,
从而
K2​≥4.
所以“K2​=3”不能作为可直接证明的目标；较合理的小目标是判定 K2​=4 还是更大。即使证明 K2​=4，它也不足以承担本论文的升层任务。

4. 四种升层杠杆逐项裁决
(a) 解决具名公开问题：目前不适用
Hejda–Steiner 的原始公开问题中，问题 (C) 明确问：

“一般二次 β 的整数 β-adic 展开前缀具有什么结构？”

同页还列出 γ(β)=1、γ(β)=0 和 cubic Pisot 情形的问题。arXiv
但 β-adic 前缀的定义是
x−i=0∑n−1​ui​βi∈βnZ[β].
这是 Z[β] 中按主理想 βnZ[β] 的同余。arXiv
现稿处理的是
j=0∑m−1​Qj​et+j​∈Qm​Z,
即普通整数环中模整数 Qm​ 的同余。两者之间没有现成的同态，也没有理由令 Qm​Z 对应 βmZ[β]。
什么会解决这一反对：
必须证明一个独立的比较定理，把 rank congruence 的有限前缀纤维嵌入或双射到 β-adic prefix classes，并证明该比较对移位和 carry 相容。
现稿没有这样的桥；fixed θ 公式也不产生它。因此不能把任何 Hejda–Steiner 问题报作可达目标。

(b) 对标准对象证明结果：有条件适用，而且这是正确方向
正确的标准对象不是数值 β-normalization本身，而是：
Pisot numeration 的 bounded zero representations 与 normalization automaton​
标准数值 normalization 保持的是
∑cj​βj
的精确数值，而现稿只保持整数 rank 的模 Qm​ 余数。Pisot 基数上的数值 normalization 可由有限 transducer 实现，但那仍是另一张映射。arXiv
真正可用的桥是前述恒等式
j<m∑​uj​et+j​=kt​um​⟺[et​,…,et+m−1​,−kt​]U​=0.
它把每个窗口碰撞变成标准 U-零表示。bounded zero representations 的正则性已有标准理论。arXiv
但是不要声称
lim​Z/um​Z≅ZU​.
新的 U-adic 工作明确指出，Pisot carries 可向两个方向传播，所得 ZU​ 一般不是 profinite group；而有限群逆极限必为 profinite。因此这种最直观的模 um​ 逆极限识别在拓扑上不可能成立。arXiv 初值的选择还会改变环面映射是 tiling、covering 还是不满射，进一步说明 Qj​ 的非标准初值不能被静默忽略。arXiv
所以 (b) 的可行版本正是定理 A，而不是“把 rank-fold 改称 U-adic normalization”。

(c) 删除真实假设：剩余杠杆很弱
Pisot 假设在 simple-Parry 差分图、aperture-two 三分中已经被真实删除；这是现稿的有效增量。
剩下可删的主要是假设 simple-Parry。但一般 eventually-Parry 或一般 sofic β-shift 中，canonical colex rank 通常不再是单一标量权重
∑xj​Qj​;
它会依赖 automaton state。此时差分商需要附加语言状态，容易退化回标准 pair graph。即使技术上扩展成功，也未必保留本文最有特色的 Toeplitz arithmetic quotient。
因此：


“simple-Parry → eventually-Parry”成功概率不低；


但它很可能只是 state-augmented classical construction；


单独不足以升层。


负共轭二次室的 ℓ=2 也不是 hypothesis removal，而是结论补全。

(d) sharpness / matching bound：这里最适用
这是当前唯一真实强杠杆。
现稿的一般上界是指数级状态界，而 Bassino 族只证明某个随基数变化的线性下界。固定 θ 公式会证明：
ℓcau​(θ,m)≍m
即使基数、次数和字母表全部固定，深度仍无界。
但这只排除了 Oθ​(1)；它尚未说明现稿指数界的正确增长阶。
真正的 matching result 必须是：
ℓcau​(U,m)=OU​(m)
对全部固定的相关 Pisot numeration 成立，同时固定 θ 给出
ℓcau​(θ,m)∼m.
这才是 sharpness，而不是再增加一个 exact example。

5. 即使首选定理证明后，最强的审稿反对意见
即使定理 A 完整成立，最难的一条反对仍会是：

论文所量化的仍是人为选择的 cyclic finite-section normalization 的 decoder depth；它是否揭示了 Pisot numeration 或 β-dynamics 的内在现象，而非某种特殊坐标编码的复杂性？

这一反对很强，因为：


阈值以上的 Yβ,m​ 共轭于 full shift；


Fischer 状态数和 Markov 阶是在固定输出字母表下的 presentation complexity，而非共轭不变量；


现稿明确不主张 numerical β-normalization、signed digits、natural extension 或 tile 结论。


结论部分也承认，现有机器不处理 β-value equality、一般高次非 simple-Parry 基数或标准 Pisot substitution interfaces。


真正消除这一反对所需的结果
定理 A 之后还应当给出一个比较推论，但不必再是一整篇新理论。最低合格形式是：

线性常数 CU​ 可以由标准 bounded-zero automaton 的某个 canonical synchronization exponent 或 zero-preservation constants Kc​(U) 有效控制；并且固定 θ 的精确终端路径同时给出该标准 normalization invariant 的精确线性下界。

也就是说，最终应出现一个不再提 Fold 的结论，例如：

对固定 Pisot numeration U，所有宽度 m 的、逐位重叠的 bounded zero-representation strips，要么可泵成周期零表示，要么在 OU​(m) 步内同步；该阶在 Uθ 上为 Θ(m)。

这样，rank-fold 会成为发现和证明标准零表示定理的工具，而不是论文的最终对象。
若做不到这一比较，最强反对意见不会彻底消失；但定理 A 的统一性和 matching bound 仍可能足以使论文上升一个层级。若只加入固定 θ 公式，该反对意见几乎原封不动地保留。

最终 go/no-go


把固定 θ 公式作为现稿加强：Go。


把固定 θ 公式单独当作升层工程：No-go。


把“固定 Pisot numeration 下 O(m) 上界 + 固定 θ 的精确 Θ(m) 下界”作为唯一升层目标：Go，但成功概率仅约 0.35。


若无法建立新的重叠零表示条带压缩引理，则应接受结构性封顶：本稿可以变得更完整、更漂亮，但不能仅靠同类 exact-depth 计算再上一个层级。


最后，这一层级判断仍以当前 Lemma 5.3、6.7、6.8 的全参数证明最终可签署为前提。现稿自己正确声明脚本只是 regression 与廉价证伪，而不是统一证明证书。

 succeeded in 238ms:
总裁定
不可达到。
以现稿的事实边界为限，目前没有一项已定位到原始出处的、同层级的命名公开问题，能够同时通过以下四个门槛：


结论落在既有标准对象上，而非仅落在本文的循环语言秩映射上；


不把有限 Parry 数据、simple-Parry 性或待判定的自动机结构预先作为输入；


参数一致证明可由现有整数递推、Toeplitz 同余与碰撞图完成；


存在不依赖“先猜定理再枚举支持”的决定性证伪检验。


现稿最强的通用工具——有限差分碰撞图——是在 dβ​(1) 已知有限、Qj​ 已可生成之后，精确计算本文特定滑动码的未来解码长度和双边单射性；它并不产生 β-数值等价、任意自动机的识别结构或未知 Parry 数据。  而且现稿明确区分秩同余与 Z[β] 中的数值正规化。
因此，最值得投入的方向不是再寻找术语上相近的公开猜想，而是证明一个显著加强现稿、但应诚实标为本文映射内部定理的固定基数结果。

一、经原始出处核对但不具备非循环攻击路线的命名问题
1. Ultimate Periodicity Problem for Linear Numeration Systems
出处。 É. Charlier、A. Massuir、M. Rigo、E. Rowland，Ultimate periodicity problem for linear numeration systems，International Journal of Algebra and Computation 32 (2022), 561–596，DOI 10.1142/S0218196722500254；预印本 arXiv:2007.08147v2，Problem 1。问题的输入是线性数系 U 及接受 repU​(X) 的任意 DFA，要求判定 X⊆N 是否最终周期。Eric Rowland
为什么现有工具不能攻击。 对 Pisot 数系，这一判定问题已可借助加法的 U-可识别性和相应一阶理论处理；开放部分恰在更一般的数系及任意输入 DFA，而不是本文某一固定 rank-fold 的纤维结构。Eric Rowland 现有碰撞图的状态是长度 m−1 的数字差分，边条件只有一个固定 Toeplitz 同余。它没有承载任意 DFA 的转移幺半群，也没有把“最终周期”转换成“本文滑动码单射”的构造。
真正需要的桥接引理将是：

对任意输入 (U,A)，有效构造一个 Parry 基数与窗口，使 A 所识别集合最终周期，当且仅当相应循环秩滑动码满足某一碰撞图性质。

这不是技术性补缝，而是一个能够把任意正则语言的算术语义编码进单一递推 Toeplitz 核的归约。目前工具完全没有这一表达能力。
决定性证伪。 固定同一个 U，取两个结构不同的 DFA，分别识别最终周期与非最终周期集合。任何只依赖 Qj​、m 和数字差分字母表而不实质编码 DFA 转移结构的候选归约都会对二者生成同一碰撞系统，因而立即失败。

2. Bassino 的高次数 simple β-number 分类问题
出处。 F. Bassino，Beta-expansions for cubic Pisot numbers，LATIN 2002，LNCS 2286，141–152，DOI 10.1007/3-540-45995-2_17。Bassino 在 Proposition 2 之后明确指出：对更高次数的 simple β-numbers，给出类似三次情形的系数分类仍是开放问题；定位为原文第 12 页。SciSpace 现稿参考文献亦给出了相同书目信息。
为什么现有工具不能攻击。 该问题要求从最小多项式或代数共轭数据判断 dβ​(1) 是否有限，并在有限时确定其形状。本文的 Qj​ 递推、秩区间双射和碰撞图却以有限字 t1​⋯tp​=dβ​(1) 为起点。因而直接套用这些工具等于把“β 是否 simple-Parry”作为先验输入。
非循环的最小桥接引理必须是以下二者之一：
由最小多项式有效给出一个终止界 B(P),
使得 greedy orbit 在 B(P) 步内未终止便永不终止；或者构造一个不预设周期数据的有限状态轨道判据。现有最近倍数分离和滑动核消失均不控制 Tβn​(1)，也不提供这样的终止界。
决定性证伪。 对一个未预先给出 dβ​(1) 的四次候选多项式运行拟议方法。若在建立 Qj​、模数或状态图之前仍必须输入一个有限 Parry 字，路线即属循环，直接否决。

3. 为什么一般的 sliding-block decoder 文献也不提供可接管的公开问题
有限状态编码的 decoder-window 上界并非空白领域。Ashley 已证明一般受限系统编码存在与图状态数线性相关的 sliding-block decoder window 上界，并给出相应一般下界。IEEE Xplore 因而本文能够形成的新贡献只能是某个算术生成的码族中精确的 right-closing delay／inverse anticipation，而不是一般 decoder 的存在性或一般有限性。

二、真正可达的内部定理候选
排名 1：单一固定二进制三次 Pisot 基数中的精确无界解码深度
成功概率：0.82。
预期层级：TIER‑2 下沿；若作为孤立短文则更接近 TIER‑3 上沿。
综合排名：1。
这是当前工具最有希望产生的最强新定理。它严格加强现稿的三次无界性：现稿需要让基数 βn​、数字字母表大小和窗口同时变化。 下面的目标固定一个基数、固定二进制字母表，只让窗口变化。
可投稿的标准术语定理陈述

定理（固定二进制三次基数的精确 right-closing delay）。
设 θ 为
x3−2x2+x−1
的唯一实根。则 θ∈(1,2) 是三次 simple-Parry Pisot 数，并且
dθ​(1)=11010∞.
令 Qm​ 为 greedy θ-shift 的长度 m 语言复杂度，并令
Φθ,m​ 为由长度 m 合法词的 colexicographic rank 模 Qm​
所定义的滑动块码。则：
Q0​=1,Q1​=2,Q2​=4,Q3​=7,Qr+4​=Qr+3​+Qr+2​+Qr​.
此外：
Φθ,2​=id,
Φθ,3​ 非单射，且其唯一非平凡双边纤维为
{0Z,1Z};
对每个 m≥4，Φθ,m​ 是从二进制全移位到其有限型像的拓扑共轭，并且其最优未来解码窗口恰为
ℓcau​(θ,m)=2⌊2m​⌋−1.​
等价地，逆码具有零记忆和精确预见
2⌊2m​⌋−2.
特别地，一个固定的二进制三次 simple-Parry Pisot 基数已经具有无界的最优未来解码深度。

现有工具能够直接承担的部分
θ 的 Pisot 性和 dθ​(1)=11010∞ 可直接验证：多项式仅有一个实根，另外两个根为共轭复根且模为 θ−1/2<1；关系
1=θ−1+θ−2+θ−4
及 Parry 后缀条件给出 greedy 展开。由此得到上述 Qr​ 递推。
随后，现稿 Theorem 6.2 已经把最优未来解码长度精确化为坏初态在有限差分碰撞图中的最长生存长度，且把非单射等价化为可达有向环。 因而无需建立任何新的符号动力学框架；新工作完全归结为一个固定四阶递推的参数一致有界 carry 分类。
仍缺的最小桥接引理
所需的核心不是重新假设定理，而是以下独立整数命题。

四步 carry 分类引理。
设
St​=j=0∑m−1​Qj​et+j​,ej​∈{−1,0,1}.
对四个平移行作递推消元
St​−St+1​−St+2​−St+4​.
则所有内部系数消失；在 m 充分大时，尾端三个数字及相应整数 carry 只能落入一个与 m 无关的有限状态集。该 carry 图是无环的，其非零终端路径按 m 的奇偶性具有唯一的最长类型，长度分别为 m−1 与 m−2。

它不假设 ℓcau​ 的值，也不假设单射；它分类的是消元后所有可能的整数 carry 状态。证明完成后，目标公式只是碰撞图定理的一行推论。
最短证明路线
第一步验证 Parry 数据和四阶 Q-递推。第二步建立尾端最近倍数分离：对有限个
uQm−3​+vQm−2​+wQm−1​,u,v,w∈{−1,0,1},
给出距 Qm​Z 的统一下界。第三步由四行消元得到固定 carry 状态图。第四步按奇偶性归纳分类其全部非零终端路径，并写出达到最长路径的显式差分词。第五步调用现稿的精确碰撞判据。
最便宜且有决定性的证伪测试
先不证明任何全参数命题，只对消元所得的候选 carry 状态作符号穷尽：状态包含三个边界数字和整数 carry；使用递推比值的向外舍入区间验证每一条可能转移。若出现拟议有限状态集之外的一个合法状态，或出现非零有向环，则整条路线立即否决。
作为有限预检，本轮独立枚举了完整碰撞图。得到：
mℓcau​​43​53​65​75​87​97​109​119​1211​1311​1413​1513​1615​​
并在 m=3 找到常差分环 et​≡1，即
1+2+4=Q3​=7.
这些有限结果与目标公式完全一致，但不构成参数一致证明。
影响判断
该结论仍然属于本文自定义 rank-fold；它不解决 β-正规化、Parry 数识别或一般 decoder-window 问题。然而，与现稿“随 n 改变基数和字母表”的无界族相比，固定 θ 与固定二进制字母表消除了最明显的外生参数增长解释。因此它是实质性的结构加强，而非单纯增加一个例子。

排名 2：全部二进制三次 Pisot 基数的 aperture–delay 完全分类
成功概率：0.48。
预期层级：完整实现时为 TIER‑2；仅给若干基数则降至 TIER‑3。
综合排名：2。
可投稿的标准术语定理陈述

定理（二进制三次 Pisot rank codes 的完全分类）。
设 β∈(1,2) 为三次 Pisot 数。则其最小多项式属于有限集合
​x3−2x2+x−1,x3−x2−x−1,x3−x2−1,x3−x−1,​
相应的有限 greedy 展开分别为
1101,111,101,10001.
对每个这样的 β，存在可显式给出的有限异常集
Eβ​⊂N、整数周期 hβ​ 以及有限表
Iβ​:Z/hβ​Z→{0,1},cβ​:Z/hβ​Z→Z,
使得对所有充分大的 m：
Φβ,m​ 单射⟺Iβ​(mmodhβ​)=1,
且在单射情形
ℓcau​(β,m)=m−cβ​(mmodhβ​).
所有异常 aperture、非单射周期碰撞及最优未来解码长度均由定理中的有限表完全列出。

这里“最终周期”作用于 aperture 参数 m，不是整数集合的 ultimate-periodicity problem；二者不得混同。
现有工具能够直接承担的部分
四个最小多项式的有限性可由根的系数界直接得到：若根为
β,α,αˉ，则 1<β<2、∣α∣<1，从而迹、二阶对称和与范数只能取有限多个整数值；逐个根判定后只剩上述四项。四个 finite Parry 字均可用对应多项式恒等式及后缀条件直接验证。Bassino 的三次展开分类可作为已发表的交叉核对来源，而不必重新建立整个三次分类。SciSpace
每个基数的 Qm​ 都满足固定线性递推；现稿的 Toeplitz 系统和碰撞图随后原样适用。
仍缺的最小桥接引理
需要证明一个固定递推的 carry 稳定化定理：

对上述四个 Q-递推，存在一个与 aperture m 无关的有限 carry 图，并允许转移规则依赖 mmodhβ​；所有 sufficiently large m 的差分碰撞图长路径均由该固定图控制。

这不是把完全分类重新写成假设。它把指数级、随 m 变化的 fiber-product 图压缩成固定有限图；一旦建立，最终周期性和精确延迟表由有限图计算得出。
最短证明路线
先完成排名 1 的 1101 基数。随后分别处理：


111：递推最简单，有限枚举显示 m≥4 时延迟恒为 2；


101：三步消元应给出按 mmod3 的终端路径分类；


10001：Parry 多项式分解
x5−x4−1=(x2−x+1)(x3−x−1)
暗示必须保留模 6 的边界相位；这是整个分类中唯一真正困难的部分。


最便宜且有决定性的证伪测试
对四个基数分别构造候选固定 carry 图，而不是继续扩大原始的 3m−1 状态图。若 carry 的绝对值不能获得与 m 无关的上界，或新的状态持续随 m 出现，则“最终有限表”路线应立即放弃。
本轮独立有限检查已经给出一个重要警告：对 x3−x−1 的根，aperture m=6,…,11 单射，但 m=12,13,14 又重新非单射，m=15,16 再次单射。因此任何“达到某个阈值后永久单射”的候选都已被否决；只有保留 aperture 相位的最终周期分类仍与数据相容。

三、综合排序依据
排名 1 的优势在于：桥接引理只有一个固定递推、一个二进制差分字母表和奇偶两类终端状态；有限检验已显示极整齐的精确公式。其成功后还会把现稿的“固定代数次数但变化基数”无界性提升为“固定基数、固定字母表”无界性，概念增量最大且证明风险可控。
排名 2 的潜在影响更广，但其成败取决于 10001 基数的非单调碰撞结构能否压缩为固定相位 carry 图。当前数据支持“复杂但有限”，尚不足以支持最终周期性证明。因此其期望值低于排名 1。
第三项不列入。 向一般 simple-Parry 基数、任意 Parry 基数或任意正则数系扩张时，Parry 多项式的互补因子可具有非 cyclotomic 的复根，当前工具尚不能保证 aperture 参数上的 carry 状态稳定；此时既没有参数一致桥接，也没有比原碰撞图指数界更强的可证目标。列入第三项会违反用户规定的“标准对象桥接、非循环性、参数一致性及自身证伪测试”四项合取。

核验边界
我已亲自核验：


现稿关于 rank congruence 非 β-value normalization 的范围声明；


Theorem 6.2 的 Toeplitz／碰撞图精确公式；

θ 的多项式恒等式、Parry 字 1101、Qm​ 递推；


排名 1 在 2≤m≤16 的完整有限碰撞图；


四个二进制三次 Pisot 多项式的系数界枚举及上述有限 aperture 数据。


尚未核验为定理的是：


排名 1 的参数一致四步 carry 完备分类；


排名 2 的固定 carry 图存在性及最终周期表；


“未发现其他可达命名公开问题”依赖本轮检索所覆盖的文献数据库，不能解释为对全部未数字化文献的逻辑穷尽。


现稿自身亦明确规定计算脚本只作回归检验而非参数一致证明；上述有限新检验同样仅承担证伪筛查，不承担证明。

定理：负共轭二次族的两窗口因果长度
令 a≥b≥1，令

证明

uj​=(−1)j+1hj​.(6)

从而 u1​≥1。

buj​≥uj−2​+auj−1​−a≥1.(9)
因此归纳得到
uj​≥1(j≥0).(10)
然而 H 为多项式，故充分大的 j 应有 uj​=0，矛盾。于是 τ=0，从而 P∣R。
这里的 (6)–(10) 即所需的参数一致有界进位不变量：任何非零常数边界进位都会强制交错商进位始终为正，因而不可能在有限位置终止。它不依赖枚举，并覆盖商多项式的全部整数分支。

由 (1)，存在整数 κ0​,κ1​ 使

# Research Directive

This file is the stable Stage A directive for the paper in this
directory.  It replaces conversation-relative instructions and must be
read before every scope, inventory, theoremization, split, and audit
step.

The publication goal is to make the current article suitable for
submission to DCDS-A.  All mature theorems, definitions,
notation, constructions, and unfinished proof interfaces that are
genuinely in scope for this article must be integrated into the
manuscript with complete proofs and proper references.  The pipeline
should strengthen and close the article's theorem chain rather than
weaken the scope merely to obtain a safe review.

Results that are mathematically interesting but outside the current
article's central scope must be recorded for a separate split-paper
pipeline.  The current article may retain such material only as a
cited tool, appendix support, or brief pointer when that is necessary
for the paper's own theorem chain.

Stage A should prefer structural, publishable conclusions: rigidity,
classification, dichotomy, extremal, obstruction, budget lower bound,
stability criterion, phase transition, local-to-global failure, and
multiscale induction closure theorems.  Bad examples should be used as
structure drivers: isolate the worst obstruction, prove it falls into
a small classifiable skeleton, and close the argument through the
appropriate decomposition, gluing obstruction, curvature certificate,
finite-budget lower bound, or induction contradiction.

Cross-resolution quantities must not be treated as genuine invariants
by naive truncation.  Such quantities require descent through a
fold-aware gauge, a stable inverse system, or an explicitly proved
equivalent mechanism.

## Deepening Delta — metallic/beta-family threshold classification (scouting)
Beyond the golden-case sharp three-window conjugacy, prove the THRESHOLD FUNCTION across the family: for every quadratic Pisot base beta (metallic means A>=1), determine the sharp window threshold m*(beta) at which the sliding-window fold becomes a topological conjugacy onto its SFT, and prove the golden mean is the UNIQUE beta achieving m*=3 (extremal), with an entropy / dynamical-zeta invariant explaining the extremality. State hypotheses explicitly; keep the golden result as the sharp special case.
Machine verify (NEW gate): numeric — for a family of quadratic Pisot beta, compute block-language counts |L_n(Y_m^beta)| and locate m*(beta); confirm golden is the unique m*=3 and tabulate m*(beta) with a counterexample search. Script+output under artifacts/. Lean: the golden m=3 block-bijection identity |L_n|=2^{n+m-1} is finite/tractable.
Order: deepen -> machine-verify -> polish -> then venue (ETDS / Nonlinearity). Do not pre-downshift.

# Stage A Scope Contract

Paper directory: `D:\omega\automath\papers\publication\2026_sharp_three_window_threshold_fibonacci_conjugacy_dcds`

Target journal: DCDS-A.

This contract fixes the Stage A scope for the DCDS-A article. It is a contract for what the article must prove, what it may cite or use only as support, and what must be deferred to sister-paper pipelines. It is not a referee report and it does not authorize weakening, deleting, demoting, renaming, or moving theorem environments.

## Central Research Question

For the canonical finite-window Fibonacci stabilization map `Fold_m : {0,1}^m -> X_m`, where `X_m` is the golden-mean legal language and `Fold_m` selects the unique length-`m` admissible representative with the same Fibonacci valuation modulo `F_{m+2}`, determine exactly when the sliding stabilized-window factor
`Phi_m : ({0,1}^Z, sigma) -> (Y_m, sigma)` becomes a finite-memory topological conjugacy onto a subshift of finite type, classify the unique failure below threshold, identify the intrinsic right-resolving presentation of the image, and explain why the golden two-window obstruction is exceptional inside the metallic family.

The article owns the sharp `m=3` threshold, the arithmetic decoder at `m=3`, the `m>=3` finite-memory two-sided conjugacy, the full `m=2` branch-locus classification, the right Fischer cover identification of `Y_m`, and the metallic two-window rigidity theorem. Quantitative residue, Fourier, second-moment, entropy, Sturmian discrepancy, and transport statements are in scope only insofar as they support or structurally exploit this conjugacy theorem.

## Target-Journal Publication Bar

For DCDS-A, the paper must read as a structural symbolic-dynamics article with arithmetic content, not as a physics-motivation paper and not as a broad enumeration or anomaly survey. The minimum publication bar is:

- A self-contained theorem chain proving the exact phase transition:
  `m=2` has one branch orbit pair and all other path-space fibers are singleton; every `m>=3` has a finite-memory conjugacy onto an SFT; no intermediate degeneration exists.
- An explicit arithmetic proof of `thm:block-invertibility` with `lem:m3-decoder` as the visible core, rather than a generic resolving-theory corollary.
- A complete proof of `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`, including local decoder `cor:explicit-decoder`, SFT memory bound, invariant measure transport, fixed-point/zeta consequences, and entropy preservation.
- A complete proof that the natural overlap presentation is the right Fischer cover of `Y_m` with `2^{m-1}` forced states, follower separation, intrinsic synchronization, and minimality among right-resolving presentations.
- A complete metallic-family comparison proving that `A=1` is the unique constant-type metallic value for which two-window normalization fails to be a sliding-block conjugacy.
- Clean scope positioning against sister papers so that duplicate-looking fiber arithmetic, gauge anomaly, Berstel-adder normalization, Sturmian discrepancy, and rotation TV material is intentionally cited or placed as support, not silently re-owned.
- Correct DCDS-A-facing metadata and framing: cover letter target, title alignment, MSC classification, bibliography naming, and introduction order must no longer carry Nonlinearity/JPA inheritance.

## Definitions, Notation, and Constructions Owned by This Paper

The paper depends on the following project definitions and notation.

- Fibonacci sequence: `F_1=F_2=1`, `F_{n+2}=F_{n+1}+F_n`.
- Golden-mean legal language `X_m = {x in {0,1}^m : x_i x_{i+1}=0}` with label `def:Xm`.
- Fibonacci valuation `Val_m(omega)=sum_{k=1}^m omega_k F_{k+1}` with label `def:valuation`.
- Finite-window Zeckendorf range `Val_m|_{X_m} : X_m -> {0,...,F_{m+2}-1}` with label `prop:finite-window-range`.
- Canonical stabilization `Fold_m(omega) = (Val_m|_{X_m})^{-1}(Val_m(omega) mod F_{m+2})` with label `def:fold`.
- Fiber notation `F_m(x)=Fold_m^{-1}({x})`, `d_m(x)=|F_m(x)|`, residue-indexed counts `d_m^sharp(r)`, and shifted-weight counts `tilde d_m(r)`.
- Sliding stabilized-window factor `Phi_m`, image shift `Y_m`, and finite-block map `Phi_{m,n}` with labels `def:Phi` and `thm:block-invertibility`.
- Overlap graph `G_m` with vertices `V_m={0,1}^{m-1}` and edge labels `ell(u,c)=Fold_m(u_1,...,u_{m-1},c)` with label `def:overlap-graph`.
- Explicit local inverse `psi_m` of memory `0` and anticipation `m-1` with labels `cor:explicit-decoder` and `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`.
- Two-window branch point `(00)^Z` in `Y_2` and branch preimage pair `{0^Z,1^Z}` with label `thm:m2-branch-locus`.
- Metallic recurrence `Q_0^{(A)}=1`, `Q_1^{(A)}=A+1`, `Q_{j+2}^{(A)}=A Q_{j+1}^{(A)}+Q_j^{(A)}`, legal language `X_m^{(A)}`, valuation `Val_{A,m}`, normalization `Fold_{A,m}`, and sliding map `Phi_{A,2}` in `thm:metallic-two-window-rigidity`.
- Fischer-cover follower language `F^+(u)` for vertices of `G_m` and intrinsic synchronization of length-`m` labels in `thm:fischer-cover-stabilized-image`.

## Must Prove Inside This Paper

The paper must prove the following in its own theorem chain.

- Finite-window canonicality of `Fold_m`: well-definedness, idempotence on `X_m`, surjectivity, and fiber partition in `thm:fold-suite`, with the Lean tag `paper_fibonacci_stabilization_fold_suite` preserved.
- The exact finite-state lift `thm:finite-state-lift`: `Y_m` is presented by `G_m`; `G_m` is right-resolving and left-resolving; and `Phi_m` has degree at most `2^{m-1}`.
- The explicit arithmetic decoder `lem:m3-decoder`, including the residue formula `q=r_0-r_1-r_2 mod 5`, the exceptional branch `q=2`, and separation by `(r_0,r_2)`.
- The sharp block-invertibility theorem `thm:block-invertibility` for every `m>=3`, including the `m=3` decoder and the `m>=4` congruence mechanism
  `q_t = r_t-r_{t+1}-r_{t+2} = a_t+a_{t+1}+F_m a_{t+m+1} mod F_{m+2}`.
- The disjointness skeleton for `m>=4`: `{0,1,2}` versus `{F_m,F_m+1,F_m+2}` and the inequality `F_m+2 < F_m+F_{m+1}=F_{m+2}`.
- The explicit sliding-block decoder above threshold `cor:explicit-decoder`, with memory `0` and anticipation `m-1`.
- Sharpness counterexamples `prop:low-window-counterexamples`, including the full `m=2` sequence-level failure and the `m=3,n=2` block pair `0001` and `0110`.
- Complete two-window branch-locus classification `thm:m2-branch-locus`: the only nontrivial path-space fiber is `{0^Z,1^Z}` over `(00)^Z`.
- Exact `m=2` conditional-entropy and KL-defect formulas in `cor:m2-defect-formulas`, plus asymptotic sufficiency off the branch and mod-zero invertibility in `cor:m2-asymptotic-sufficiency` and `cor:m2-mod-zero`.
- The one-sided and two-sided conjugacy consequences: `cor:one-sided-complexity`, `cor:one-sided-conjugacy`, and `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`.
- The finite-type closure claim in `thm:two-sided-conjugacy(iii)`, including the decoded-block overlap condition and the memory bound.
- Standard dynamical consequences `prop:conjugacy-consequences`: zeta function, fixed-point counts, invariant measure transport, and transported ergodic properties.
- Entropy preservation `prop:measure-entropy-preservation` and `cor:entropy-preservation`, with `h_top(Y_m)=log 2`.
- A stand-alone phase-transition theorem or equivalent framing packaging: `m=2` unique branch orbit pair, `m>=3` finite-memory SFT conjugacy, and no intermediate degeneration.
- Metallic two-window rigidity `thm:metallic-two-window-rigidity`, explicitly stating that `A=1` is the unique metallic parameter for which two-window normalization fails to be a sliding-block conjugacy.
- The arithmetic obstruction in the metallic proof: for `A>=2`, `{0}` and `{A-1,A}` are disjoint, while at `A=1` they collapse to `{0}` and `{0,1}`.
- Fischer-cover theorem `thm:fischer-cover-stabilized-image`: length-`m` labels are intrinsically synchronizing, follower languages are separated, `G_m` is the right Fischer cover, and every right-resolving presentation has at least `2^{m-1}` states.
- Residue characterization `thm:fiber-residue`, Fourier formula `thm:fiber-dft`, affine transfer `thm:affine-transfer`, and second-moment recurrence `prop:second-moment-recurrence` as arithmetic-side outputs of `Fold_m`, not as co-headline results.
- If the human decision is to re-include `sections_jpa/06_measurement_theory.tex`, prove its transport consequences as direct corollaries of the conjugacy: block-level Blackwell equivalence, KL/TV identities under `Phi_{m,n}`, path-space relative-entropy-rate identity, continuous-potential pressure/equilibrium-state transport, and finite-range interaction translation `phi -> tilde phi` of range `r+m-1`.
- If the human decision is to promote `sections_jpa/A6_tv_extensions.tex` golden optimality, state it as a rigidity/extremal companion result and avoid treating the observational free-energy functional as a thermodynamic free energy of an interacting system.

## Failure Modes to Control

- Do not let general sofic resolving theory obscure the arithmetic decoder; `lem:m3-decoder` and the `m>=4` congruence disjointness are the core mechanisms.
- Do not present the `m=2` collapse as a generic warning. It must be the whole branch locus: exactly `{0^Z,1^Z}` over `(00)^Z`.
- Do not let fixed-window fiber multiplicity be mistaken for path-space information loss above threshold. The paper must distinguish isolated-window fibers from overlapping-window conjugacy.
- Do not treat cross-resolution objects such as `G_m`, `S_m`, truncation-stabilization mismatch, or descendant statistics as invariants under naive truncation. They require fold-aware gauge descent, stable inverse-system descent, or an equivalent proved mechanism; otherwise they are split-paper material.
- Do not import conjecture-dependent anomaly recurrences or empirical closed forms into this article.
- Do not allow the source-layer quasicrystal or Sturmian motivation to lead the paper structurally; DCDS-A should see the threshold/conjugacy theorem first.
- Do not overpromote fiber arithmetic, Fourier formulas, second moments, or TV estimates into co-headline results. They are arithmetic-side and transport-side supports.
- Do not duplicate the m=5 example in both main text and appendix in a final DCDS-A version.
- Do not duplicate labels from excluded appendices, especially `prop:uniform-collision`, `eq:col-unif-second-moment`, or `tab:sanity-fibers`.
- Do not use `observational free energy` language in a way that suggests a thermodynamic free energy of an interacting system unless the thermodynamic object is explicitly defined.
- Do not leave wrong-target metadata visible: Nonlinearity cover letter, JPA bibliography scaffolding, or `sections_jpa` naming in referee-facing comments.

## Structural Bad Examples and Their Role

The paper should use bad examples as structure drivers, not as loose warnings.

- `m=2`: `0^Z` and `1^Z` both map to the all-`00` output. This is the complete branch locus and the support of the entropy/KL defect.
- `m=3,n=2`: `0001` and `0110` both map to `(000,001)`. This proves the finite-block threshold `n>=m` is sharp even at the first invertible aperture.
- `m=3` decoder: the exceptional residue branch `q=2` must remain explicit and separated by `(r_0,r_2)`.
- `m>=4` decoder: the possible values of `q_t` split into the finite-budget disjoint alternatives `{0,1,2}` and `{F_m,F_m+1,F_m+2}`, forcing the boundary bit.
- Metallic `A>=2`: local solvability is forced by the disjoint alternatives `{0}` and `{A-1,A}`; the golden case collapses this distinction and produces the branch.
- Gauge anomaly `110 -> 001`: if mentioned, it must be described only as an obstruction to naive cross-resolution truncation, not as a DCDS-A invariant.

This DCDS-A article owns the sharp three-window threshold, the finite-memory conjugacy of the stabilized-window factor, the two-window branch-locus classification, the Fischer-cover minimality statement, and the metallic two-window rigidity theorem. It cites the Berstel-adder paper for transducer-side Zeckendorf normalization, the TAMS fiber-arithmetic paper for detailed finite-window fiber thermodynamics, the spectral-fingerprints paper for truncation-stabilization gauge anomalies and cross-resolution discrepancy statistics, and the ETDS Sturmian/rotation papers for source-law discrepancy and folded-histogram transport. The present article includes only those residue, Fourier, second-moment, entropy, and TV estimates that directly support or are transported by the conjugacy theorem.

# Research Directive

This file is the stable Stage A directive for the paper in this
directory.  It replaces conversation-relative instructions and must be
read before every scope, inventory, theoremization, split, and audit
step.

The publication goal is to make the current article suitable for
submission to DCDS-A.  All mature theorems, definitions,
notation, constructions, and unfinished proof interfaces that are
genuinely in scope for this article must be integrated into the
manuscript with complete proofs and proper references.  The pipeline
should strengthen and close the article's theorem chain rather than
weaken the scope merely to obtain a safe review.

Results that are mathematically interesting but outside the current
article's central scope must be recorded for a separate split-paper
pipeline.  The current article may retain such material only as a
cited tool, appendix support, or brief pointer when that is necessary
for the paper's own theorem chain.

Stage A should prefer structural, publishable conclusions: rigidity,
classification, dichotomy, extremal, obstruction, budget lower bound,
stability criterion, phase transition, local-to-global failure, and
multiscale induction closure theorems.  Bad examples should be used as
structure drivers: isolate the worst obstruction, prove it falls into
a small classifiable skeleton, and close the argument through the
appropriate decomposition, gluing obstruction, curvature certificate,
finite-budget lower bound, or induction contradiction.

Cross-resolution quantities must not be treated as genuine invariants
by naive truncation.  Such quantities require descent through a
fold-aware gauge, a stable inverse system, or an explicitly proved
equivalent mechanism.

## Deepening Delta — metallic/beta-family threshold classification (scouting)
Beyond the golden-case sharp three-window conjugacy, prove the THRESHOLD FUNCTION across the family: for every quadratic Pisot base beta (metallic means A>=1), determine the sharp window threshold m*(beta) at which the sliding-window fold becomes a topological conjugacy onto its SFT, and prove the golden mean is the UNIQUE beta achieving m*=3 (extremal), with an entropy / dynamical-zeta invariant explaining the extremality. State hypotheses explicitly; keep the golden result as the sharp special case.
Machine verify (NEW gate): numeric — for a family of quadratic Pisot beta, compute block-language counts |L_n(Y_m^beta)| and locate m*(beta); confirm golden is the unique m*=3 and tabulate m*(beta) with a counterexample search. Script+output under artifacts/. Lean: the golden m=3 block-bijection identity |L_n|=2^{n+m-1} is finite/tractable.
Order: deepen -> machine-verify -> polish -> then venue (ETDS / Nonlinearity). Do not pre-downshift.

# Stage A Scope Contract

Paper directory: `D:\omega\automath\papers\publication\2026_sharp_three_window_threshold_fibonacci_conjugacy_dcds`

Target journal: DCDS-A.

This contract fixes the Stage A scope for the DCDS-A article. It is a contract for what the article must prove, what it may cite or use only as support, and what must be deferred to sister-paper pipelines. It is not a referee report and it does not authorize weakening, deleting, demoting, renaming, or moving theorem environments.

## Central Research Question

For the canonical finite-window Fibonacci stabilization map `Fold_m : {0,1}^m -> X_m`, where `X_m` is the golden-mean legal language and `Fold_m` selects the unique length-`m` admissible representative with the same Fibonacci valuation modulo `F_{m+2}`, determine exactly when the sliding stabilized-window factor
`Phi_m : ({0,1}^Z, sigma) -> (Y_m, sigma)` becomes a finite-memory topological conjugacy onto a subshift of finite type, classify the unique failure below threshold, identify the intrinsic right-resolving presentation of the image, and explain why the golden two-window obstruction is exceptional inside the metallic family.

The article owns the sharp `m=3` threshold, the arithmetic decoder at `m=3`, the `m>=3` finite-memory two-sided conjugacy, the full `m=2` branch-locus classification, the right Fischer cover identification of `Y_m`, and the metallic two-window rigidity theorem. Quantitative residue, Fourier, second-moment, entropy, Sturmian discrepancy, and transport statements are in scope only insofar as they support or structurally exploit this conjugacy theorem.

## Target-Journal Publication Bar

For DCDS-A, the paper must read as a structural symbolic-dynamics article with arithmetic content, not as a physics-motivation paper and not as a broad enumeration or anomaly survey. The minimum publication bar is:

- A self-contained theorem chain proving the exact phase transition:
  `m=2` has one branch orbit pair and all other path-space fibers are singleton; every `m>=3` has a finite-memory conjugacy onto an SFT; no intermediate degeneration exists.
- An explicit arithmetic proof of `thm:block-invertibility` with `lem:m3-decoder` as the visible core, rather than a generic resolving-theory corollary.
- A complete proof of `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`, including local decoder `cor:explicit-decoder`, SFT memory bound, invariant measure transport, fixed-point/zeta consequences, and entropy preservation.
- A complete proof that the natural overlap presentation is the right Fischer cover of `Y_m` with `2^{m-1}` forced states, follower separation, intrinsic synchronization, and minimality among right-resolving presentations.
- A complete metallic-family comparison proving that `A=1` is the unique constant-type metallic value for which two-window normalization fails to be a sliding-block conjugacy.
- Clean scope positioning against sister papers so that duplicate-looking fiber arithmetic, gauge anomaly, Berstel-adder normalization, Sturmian discrepancy, and rotation TV material is intentionally cited or placed as support, not silently re-owned.
- Correct DCDS-A-facing metadata and framing: cover letter target, title alignment, MSC classification, bibliography naming, and introduction order must no longer carry Nonlinearity/JPA inheritance.

## Definitions, Notation, and Constructions Owned by This Paper

The paper depends on the following project definitions and notation.

- Fibonacci sequence: `F_1=F_2=1`, `F_{n+2}=F_{n+1}+F_n`.
- Golden-mean legal language `X_m = {x in {0,1}^m : x_i x_{i+1}=0}` with label `def:Xm`.
- Fibonacci valuation `Val_m(omega)=sum_{k=1}^m omega_k F_{k+1}` with label `def:valuation`.
- Finite-window Zeckendorf range `Val_m|_{X_m} : X_m -> {0,...,F_{m+2}-1}` with label `prop:finite-window-range`.
- Canonical stabilization `Fold_m(omega) = (Val_m|_{X_m})^{-1}(Val_m(omega) mod F_{m+2})` with label `def:fold`.
- Fiber notation `F_m(x)=Fold_m^{-1}({x})`, `d_m(x)=|F_m(x)|`, residue-indexed counts `d_m^sharp(r)`, and shifted-weight counts `tilde d_m(r)`.
- Sliding stabilized-window factor `Phi_m`, image shift `Y_m`, and finite-block map `Phi_{m,n}` with labels `def:Phi` and `thm:block-invertibility`.
- Overlap graph `G_m` with vertices `V_m={0,1}^{m-1}` and edge labels `ell(u,c)=Fold_m(u_1,...,u_{m-1},c)` with label `def:overlap-graph`.
- Explicit local inverse `psi_m` of memory `0` and anticipation `m-1` with labels `cor:explicit-decoder` and `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`.
- Two-window branch point `(00)^Z` in `Y_2` and branch preimage pair `{0^Z,1^Z}` with label `thm:m2-branch-locus`.
- Metallic recurrence `Q_0^{(A)}=1`, `Q_1^{(A)}=A+1`, `Q_{j+2}^{(A)}=A Q_{j+1}^{(A)}+Q_j^{(A)}`, legal language `X_m^{(A)}`, valuation `Val_{A,m}`, normalization `Fold_{A,m}`, and sliding map `Phi_{A,2}` in `thm:metallic-two-window-rigidity`.
- Fischer-cover follower language `F^+(u)` for vertices of `G_m` and intrinsic synchronization of length-`m` labels in `thm:fischer-cover-stabilized-image`.

## Must Prove Inside This Paper

The paper must prove the following in its own theorem chain.

- Finite-window canonicality of `Fold_m`: well-definedness, idempotence on `X_m`, surjectivity, and fiber partition in `thm:fold-suite`, with the Lean tag `paper_fibonacci_stabilization_fold_suite` preserved.
- The exact finite-state lift `thm:finite-state-lift`: `Y_m` is presented by `G_m`; `G_m` is right-resolving and left-resolving; and `Phi_m` has degree at most `2^{m-1}`.
- The explicit arithmetic decoder `lem:m3-decoder`, including the residue formula `q=r_0-r_1-r_2 mod 5`, the exceptional branch `q=2`, and separation by `(r_0,r_2)`.
- The sharp block-invertibility theorem `thm:block-invertibility` for every `m>=3`, including the `m=3` decoder and the `m>=4` congruence mechanism
  `q_t = r_t-r_{t+1}-r_{t+2} = a_t+a_{t+1}+F_m a_{t+m+1} mod F_{m+2}`.
- The disjointness skeleton for `m>=4`: `{0,1,2}` versus `{F_m,F_m+1,F_m+2}` and the inequality `F_m+2 < F_m+F_{m+1}=F_{m+2}`.
- The explicit sliding-block decoder above threshold `cor:explicit-decoder`, with memory `0` and anticipation `m-1`.
- Sharpness counterexamples `prop:low-window-counterexamples`, including the full `m=2` sequence-level failure and the `m=3,n=2` block pair `0001` and `0110`.
- Complete two-window branch-locus classification `thm:m2-branch-locus`: the only nontrivial path-space fiber is `{0^Z,1^Z}` over `(00)^Z`.
- Exact `m=2` conditional-entropy and KL-defect formulas in `cor:m2-defect-formulas`, plus asymptotic sufficiency off the branch and mod-zero invertibility in `cor:m2-asymptotic-sufficiency` and `cor:m2-mod-zero`.
- The one-sided and two-sided conjugacy consequences: `cor:one-sided-complexity`, `cor:one-sided-conjugacy`, and `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`.
- The finite-type closure claim in `thm:two-sided-conjugacy(iii)`, including the decoded-block overlap condition and the memory bound.
- Standard dynamical consequences `prop:conjugacy-consequences`: zeta function, fixed-point counts, invariant measure transport, and transported ergodic properties.
- Entropy preservation `prop:measure-entropy-preservation` and `cor:entropy-preservation`, with `h_top(Y_m)=log 2`.
- A stand-alone phase-transition theorem or equivalent framing packaging: `m=2` unique branch orbit pair, `m>=3` finite-memory SFT conjugacy, and no intermediate degeneration.
- Metallic two-window rigidity `thm:metallic-two-window-rigidity`, explicitly stating that `A=1` is the unique metallic parameter for which two-window normalization fails to be a sliding-block conjugacy.
- The arithmetic obstruction in the metallic proof: for `A>=2`, `{0}` and `{A-1,A}` are disjoint, while at `A=1` they collapse to `{0}` and `{0,1}`.
- Fischer-cover theorem `thm:fischer-cover-stabilized-image`: length-`m` labels are intrinsically synchronizing, follower languages are separated, `G_m` is the right Fischer cover, and every right-resolving presentation has at least `2^{m-1}` states.
- Residue characterization `thm:fiber-residue`, Fourier formula `thm:fiber-dft`, affine transfer `thm:affine-transfer`, and second-moment recurrence `prop:second-moment-recurrence` as arithmetic-side outputs of `Fold_m`, not as co-headline results.
- If the human decision is to re-include `sections_jpa/06_measurement_theory.tex`, prove its transport consequences as direct corollaries of the conjugacy: block-level Blackwell equivalence, KL/TV identities under `Phi_{m,n}`, path-space relative-entropy-rate identity, continuous-potential pressure/equilibrium-state transport, and finite-range interaction translation `phi -> tilde phi` of range `r+m-1`.
- If the human decision is to promote `sections_jpa/A6_tv_extensions.tex` golden optimality, state it as a rigidity/extremal companion result and avoid treating the observational free-energy functional as a thermodynamic free energy of an interacting system.

- `sec:superspace` cut-and-project source motivation: keep brief or move to an appendix; it must not be the opening structural section for DCDS-A.
- `sec:readout`, including `prop:induced-law`, `thm:noise-full-support`, and `prop:detector-realization`: auxiliary justification for why the full cube is the correct local domain after noisy or finite-radius readout.
- `sec:entropy-ledger`: fixed-window fiber-uniform lift and KL ledger; useful only to contrast isolated-window multiplicity with path-space invertibility.
- `sec:fiber-spectrum`: second-moment recurrence and the small enumeration table; retain as arithmetic-side evidence for the fiber structure of `Fold_m`, not as a central thermodynamic paper.
- `sec:fp:tv`: Sturmian discrepancy, boundary-layer, resolution certificate, and operational observable recovery; retain as deterministic transport along the conjugacy, not as an applied probability co-headline.
- `sections_jpa/06_measurement_theory.tex`: recommended for inclusion after human confirmation because it is the cleanest DCDS-A structural transport section; if included, it should follow the conjugacy theorem and be framed as consequences of `thm:two-sided-conjugacy`.
- `sections_jpa/A6_tv_extensions.tex`: may be promoted only if the user chooses to make golden optimality among metallic means a body-level rigidity/extremal result; otherwise it is supporting appendix material or a citation to the rotation-TV line.
- `sections_jpa/07c_generalization.tex`: at most a closing remark; do not assert a comparable uniform decoder for general Ostrowski systems.

## Out of Scope and Split-Paper Candidates

The following must not be integrated as owned DCDS-A results.

- `sections_jpa/A4_gauge_anomaly.tex`: exclude. It contains the truncation-stabilization anomaly `G_m`, worst-case anomaly, and an explicit conjecture for the uniform anomaly sequence `S_m`. Cross-resolution quantities are not invariants by naive truncation and belong to `2026_fibonacci_folding_zeckendorf_normalization_gauge_anomaly_spectral_fingerprints` unless descent through a fold-aware gauge or stable inverse system is proved.
- `sections_jpa/06_addressing.tex`: exclude. Overlap transducer synchronization, occupancy identities, and collision-limited addressing belong to the addressing/auditable-addressing line, including `2026_golden_mean_folding_stable_types_auditable_addressing` and `2026_recursive_addressing_prefix_sites_tac`. The synchronization length `T_m=m-1` may survive only as a short decoder-complexity remark.
- `sections_jpa/A5_uniform_collision.tex`: exclude. It duplicates addressing and uniform-collision benchmark material and reuses labels already present in `04b_fiber_spectrum.tex`.
- `sections_jpa/A1_example.tex`: exclude as a duplicate unless the inline `m=5` remark is removed in a later stage. Stage A does not remove either; it records the duplication.
- `sections_jpa/A2_sanity_checks.tex` and `sections_jpa/A3_uniform_benchmarks.tex`: keep at most one small table. The A3 table references `G_m` and forces the gauge-anomaly pipeline, so it must be deferred with A4.
- ETDS prefix scan-error material from the prior research ledger: out of scope. That pipeline owns prefix filtrations, scan-error profiles, Tanaka compensators, finite-state posterior presentations, boundary pressure, and the golden-mean event pressure example. None of those results should be imported into this DCDS-A article except as unrelated background memory.
- General Ostrowski/metallic finite-window decoder theory beyond `thm:metallic-two-window-rigidity`: split to the Ostrowski metallic numeration line.
- Gauge, spectral-fingerprint, moment-tower, operator-algebra, and window-6 spectral rigidity conclusions: cite only if directly relevant and do not rederive.

- Sharp `m=3` threshold for `Phi_m`.

- DCDS-A-facing structural transport consequences when derived directly from the conjugacy.

Use as cite-only or supporting background:

- `submitted_2026_canonical_zeckendorf_normalization_berstel_adder_rairo_ita`: owns classical/canonical Zeckendorf normalization, Berstel-adder state complexity, online-delay normalization, and transducer-side normalization. This paper may cite it for normalization background but must not duplicate its adders or streaming-normalization theorems.

This paper may keep only the residue/Fourier/affine-transfer/second-moment facts needed to characterize `Fold_m`.
- `2026_fibonacci_folding_zeckendorf_normalization_gauge_anomaly_spectral_fingerprints`: owns truncation-stabilization anomaly, compatible restriction, pair-shift discrepancy process, density `4/9`, asymptotic variance, rational spectrum, weighted fibers, and finite-core general criteria. This DCDS-A paper may cite it for gauge-anomaly and finite-core context but must own its direct arithmetic decoder proof.

- `submitted_2026_zeckendorf_folds_sturmian_rigidity_parry_divergence_etds`: owns Sturmian rigidity, Parry-divergence line, and Sturmian source-specific conjugacy coordinates. The DCDS-A paper may use Sturmian material only as source-law support and deterministic transport.

## Failure Modes to Control

- Do not let general sofic resolving theory obscure the arithmetic decoder; `lem:m3-decoder` and the `m>=4` congruence disjointness are the core mechanisms.
- Do not present the `m=2` collapse as a generic warning. It must be the whole branch locus: exactly `{0^Z,1^Z}` over `(00)^Z`.
- Do not let fixed-window fiber multiplicity be mistaken for path-space information loss above threshold. The paper must distinguish isolated-window fibers from overlapping-window conjugacy.
- Do not treat cross-resolution objects such as `G_m`, `S_m`, truncation-stabilization mismatch, or descendant statistics as invariants under naive truncation. They require fold-aware gauge descent, stable inverse-system descent, or an equivalent proved mechanism; otherwise they are split-paper material.
- Do not import conjecture-dependent anomaly recurrences or empirical closed forms into this article.
- Do not allow the source-layer quasicrystal or Sturmian motivation to lead the paper structurally; DCDS-A should see the threshold/conjugacy theorem first.
- Do not overpromote fiber arithmetic, Fourier formulas, second moments, or TV estimates into co-headline results. They are arithmetic-side and transport-side supports.
- Do not duplicate the m=5 example in both main text and appendix in a final DCDS-A version.
- Do not duplicate labels from excluded appendices, especially `prop:uniform-collision`, `eq:col-unif-second-moment`, or `tab:sanity-fibers`.
- Do not use `observational free energy` language in a way that suggests a thermodynamic free energy of an interacting system unless the thermodynamic object is explicitly defined.
- Do not leave wrong-target metadata visible: Nonlinearity cover letter, JPA bibliography scaffolding, or `sections_jpa` naming in referee-facing comments.

## Structural Bad Examples and Their Role

The paper should use bad examples as structure drivers, not as loose warnings.

- `m=2`: `0^Z` and `1^Z` both map to the all-`00` output. This is the complete branch locus and the support of the entropy/KL defect.
- `m=3,n=2`: `0001` and `0110` both map to `(000,001)`. This proves the finite-block threshold `n>=m` is sharp even at the first invertible aperture.
- `m=3` decoder: the exceptional residue branch `q=2` must remain explicit and separated by `(r_0,r_2)`.
- `m>=4` decoder: the possible values of `q_t` split into the finite-budget disjoint alternatives `{0,1,2}` and `{F_m,F_m+1,F_m+2}`, forcing the boundary bit.
- Metallic `A>=2`: local solvability is forced by the disjoint alternatives `{0}` and `{A-1,A}`; the golden case collapses this distinction and produces the branch.
- Gauge anomaly `110 -> 001`: if mentioned, it must be described only as an obstruction to naive cross-resolution truncation, not as a DCDS-A invariant.

## Required Positioning Paragraph for Later Manuscript Edit

The introduction should contain a stable positioning paragraph with this content:

This DCDS-A article owns the sharp three-window threshold, the finite-memory conjugacy of the stabilized-window factor, the two-window branch-locus classification, the Fischer-cover minimality statement, and the metallic two-window rigidity theorem. It cites the Berstel-adder paper for transducer-side Zeckendorf normalization, the TAMS fiber-arithmetic paper for detailed finite-window fiber thermodynamics, the spectral-fingerprints paper for truncation-stabilization gauge anomalies and cross-resolution discrepancy statistics, and the ETDS Sturmian/rotation papers for source-law discrepancy and folded-histogram transport. The present article includes only those residue, Fourier, second-moment, entropy, and TV estimates that directly support or are transported by the conjugacy theorem.

 succeeded in 181ms:
总裁定
不可达到。
以现稿的事实边界为限，目前没有一项已定位到原始出处的、同层级的命名公开问题，能够同时通过以下四个门槛：


结论落在既有标准对象上，而非仅落在本文的循环语言秩映射上；


不把有限 Parry 数据、simple-Parry 性或待判定的自动机结构预先作为输入；


参数一致证明可由现有整数递推、Toeplitz 同余与碰撞图完成；


存在不依赖“先猜定理再枚举支持”的决定性证伪检验。


现稿最强的通用工具——有限差分碰撞图——是在 dβ​(1) 已知有限、Qj​ 已可生成之后，精确计算本文特定滑动码的未来解码长度和双边单射性；它并不产生 β-数值等价、任意自动机的识别结构或未知 Parry 数据。  而且现稿明确区分秩同余与 Z[β] 中的数值正规化。
因此，最值得投入的方向不是再寻找术语上相近的公开猜想，而是证明一个显著加强现稿、但应诚实标为本文映射内部定理的固定基数结果。

一、经原始出处核对但不具备非循环攻击路线的命名问题
1. Ultimate Periodicity Problem for Linear Numeration Systems
出处。 É. Charlier、A. Massuir、M. Rigo、E. Rowland，Ultimate periodicity problem for linear numeration systems，International Journal of Algebra and Computation 32 (2022), 561–596，DOI 10.1142/S0218196722500254；预印本 arXiv:2007.08147v2，Problem 1。问题的输入是线性数系 U 及接受 repU​(X) 的任意 DFA，要求判定 X⊆N 是否最终周期。Eric Rowland
为什么现有工具不能攻击。 对 Pisot 数系，这一判定问题已可借助加法的 U-可识别性和相应一阶理论处理；开放部分恰在更一般的数系及任意输入 DFA，而不是本文某一固定 rank-fold 的纤维结构。Eric Rowland 现有碰撞图的状态是长度 m−1 的数字差分，边条件只有一个固定 Toeplitz 同余。它没有承载任意 DFA 的转移幺半群，也没有把“最终周期”转换成“本文滑动码单射”的构造。
真正需要的桥接引理将是：

对任意输入 (U,A)，有效构造一个 Parry 基数与窗口，使 A 所识别集合最终周期，当且仅当相应循环秩滑动码满足某一碰撞图性质。

这不是技术性补缝，而是一个能够把任意正则语言的算术语义编码进单一递推 Toeplitz 核的归约。目前工具完全没有这一表达能力。
决定性证伪。 固定同一个 U，取两个结构不同的 DFA，分别识别最终周期与非最终周期集合。任何只依赖 Qj​、m 和数字差分字母表而不实质编码 DFA 转移结构的候选归约都会对二者生成同一碰撞系统，因而立即失败。

2. Bassino 的高次数 simple β-number 分类问题
出处。 F. Bassino，Beta-expansions for cubic Pisot numbers，LATIN 2002，LNCS 2286，141–152，DOI 10.1007/3-540-45995-2_17。Bassino 在 Proposition 2 之后明确指出：对更高次数的 simple β-numbers，给出类似三次情形的系数分类仍是开放问题；定位为原文第 12 页。SciSpace 现稿参考文献亦给出了相同书目信息。
为什么现有工具不能攻击。 该问题要求从最小多项式或代数共轭数据判断 dβ​(1) 是否有限，并在有限时确定其形状。本文的 Qj​ 递推、秩区间双射和碰撞图却以有限字 t1​⋯tp​=dβ​(1) 为起点。因而直接套用这些工具等于把“β 是否 simple-Parry”作为先验输入。
非循环的最小桥接引理必须是以下二者之一：
由最小多项式有效给出一个终止界 B(P),
使得 greedy orbit 在 B(P) 步内未终止便永不终止；或者构造一个不预设周期数据的有限状态轨道判据。现有最近倍数分离和滑动核消失均不控制 Tβn​(1)，也不提供这样的终止界。
决定性证伪。 对一个未预先给出 dβ​(1) 的四次候选多项式运行拟议方法。若在建立 Qj​、模数或状态图之前仍必须输入一个有限 Parry 字，路线即属循环，直接否决。

3. 为什么一般的 sliding-block decoder 文献也不提供可接管的公开问题
有限状态编码的 decoder-window 上界并非空白领域。Ashley 已证明一般受限系统编码存在与图状态数线性相关的 sliding-block decoder window 上界，并给出相应一般下界。IEEE Xplore 因而本文能够形成的新贡献只能是某个算术生成的码族中精确的 right-closing delay／inverse anticipation，而不是一般 decoder 的存在性或一般有限性。

二、真正可达的内部定理候选
排名 1：单一固定二进制三次 Pisot 基数中的精确无界解码深度
成功概率：0.82。
预期层级：TIER‑2 下沿；若作为孤立短文则更接近 TIER‑3 上沿。
综合排名：1。
这是当前工具最有希望产生的最强新定理。它严格加强现稿的三次无界性：现稿需要让基数 βn​、数字字母表大小和窗口同时变化。 下面的目标固定一个基数、固定二进制字母表，只让窗口变化。
可投稿的标准术语定理陈述

定理（固定二进制三次基数的精确 right-closing delay）。
设 θ 为
x3−2x2+x−1
的唯一实根。则 θ∈(1,2) 是三次 simple-Parry Pisot 数，并且
dθ​(1)=11010∞.
令 Qm​ 为 greedy θ-shift 的长度 m 语言复杂度，并令
Φθ,m​ 为由长度 m 合法词的 colexicographic rank 模 Qm​
所定义的滑动块码。则：
Q0​=1,Q1​=2,Q2​=4,Q3​=7,Qr+4​=Qr+3​+Qr+2​+Qr​.
此外：
Φθ,2​=id,
Φθ,3​ 非单射，且其唯一非平凡双边纤维为
{0Z,1Z};
对每个 m≥4，Φθ,m​ 是从二进制全移位到其有限型像的拓扑共轭，并且其最优未来解码窗口恰为
ℓcau​(θ,m)=2⌊2m​⌋−1.​
等价地，逆码具有零记忆和精确预见
2⌊2m​⌋−2.
特别地，一个固定的二进制三次 simple-Parry Pisot 基数已经具有无界的最优未来解码深度。

现有工具能够直接承担的部分
θ 的 Pisot 性和 dθ​(1)=11010∞ 可直接验证：多项式仅有一个实根，另外两个根为共轭复根且模为 θ−1/2<1；关系
1=θ−1+θ−2+θ−4
及 Parry 后缀条件给出 greedy 展开。由此得到上述 Qr​ 递推。
随后，现稿 Theorem 6.2 已经把最优未来解码长度精确化为坏初态在有限差分碰撞图中的最长生存长度，且把非单射等价化为可达有向环。 因而无需建立任何新的符号动力学框架；新工作完全归结为一个固定四阶递推的参数一致有界 carry 分类。
仍缺的最小桥接引理
所需的核心不是重新假设定理，而是以下独立整数命题。

四步 carry 分类引理。
设
St​=j=0∑m−1​Qj​et+j​,ej​∈{−1,0,1}.
对四个平移行作递推消元
St​−St+1​−St+2​−St+4​.
则所有内部系数消失；在 m 充分大时，尾端三个数字及相应整数 carry 只能落入一个与 m 无关的有限状态集。该 carry 图是无环的，其非零终端路径按 m 的奇偶性具有唯一的最长类型，长度分别为 m−1 与 m−2。

它不假设 ℓcau​ 的值，也不假设单射；它分类的是消元后所有可能的整数 carry 状态。证明完成后，目标公式只是碰撞图定理的一行推论。
最短证明路线
第一步验证 Parry 数据和四阶 Q-递推。第二步建立尾端最近倍数分离：对有限个
uQm−3​+vQm−2​+wQm−1​,u,v,w∈{−1,0,1},
给出距 Qm​Z 的统一下界。第三步由四行消元得到固定 carry 状态图。第四步按奇偶性归纳分类其全部非零终端路径，并写出达到最长路径的显式差分词。第五步调用现稿的精确碰撞判据。
最便宜且有决定性的证伪测试
先不证明任何全参数命题，只对消元所得的候选 carry 状态作符号穷尽：状态包含三个边界数字和整数 carry；使用递推比值的向外舍入区间验证每一条可能转移。若出现拟议有限状态集之外的一个合法状态，或出现非零有向环，则整条路线立即否决。
作为有限预检，本轮独立枚举了完整碰撞图。得到：
mℓcau​​43​53​65​75​87​97​109​119​1211​1311​1413​1513​1615​​
并在 m=3 找到常差分环 et​≡1，即
1+2+4=Q3​=7.
这些有限结果与目标公式完全一致，但不构成参数一致证明。
影响判断
该结论仍然属于本文自定义 rank-fold；它不解决 β-正规化、Parry 数识别或一般 decoder-window 问题。然而，与现稿“随 n 改变基数和字母表”的无界族相比，固定 θ 与固定二进制字母表消除了最明显的外生参数增长解释。因此它是实质性的结构加强，而非单纯增加一个例子。

排名 2：全部二进制三次 Pisot 基数的 aperture–delay 完全分类
成功概率：0.48。
预期层级：完整实现时为 TIER‑2；仅给若干基数则降至 TIER‑3。
综合排名：2。
可投稿的标准术语定理陈述

定理（二进制三次 Pisot rank codes 的完全分类）。
设 β∈(1,2) 为三次 Pisot 数。则其最小多项式属于有限集合
​x3−2x2+x−1,x3−x2−x−1,x3−x2−1,x3−x−1,​
相应的有限 greedy 展开分别为
1101,111,101,10001.
对每个这样的 β，存在可显式给出的有限异常集
Eβ​⊂N、整数周期 hβ​ 以及有限表
Iβ​:Z/hβ​Z→{0,1},cβ​:Z/hβ​Z→Z,
使得对所有充分大的 m：
Φβ,m​ 单射⟺Iβ​(mmodhβ​)=1,
且在单射情形
ℓcau​(β,m)=m−cβ​(mmodhβ​).
所有异常 aperture、非单射周期碰撞及最优未来解码长度均由定理中的有限表完全列出。

这里“最终周期”作用于 aperture 参数 m，不是整数集合的 ultimate-periodicity problem；二者不得混同。
现有工具能够直接承担的部分
四个最小多项式的有限性可由根的系数界直接得到：若根为
β,α,αˉ，则 1<β<2、∣α∣<1，从而迹、二阶对称和与范数只能取有限多个整数值；逐个根判定后只剩上述四项。四个 finite Parry 字均可用对应多项式恒等式及后缀条件直接验证。Bassino 的三次展开分类可作为已发表的交叉核对来源，而不必重新建立整个三次分类。SciSpace
每个基数的 Qm​ 都满足固定线性递推；现稿的 Toeplitz 系统和碰撞图随后原样适用。
仍缺的最小桥接引理
需要证明一个固定递推的 carry 稳定化定理：

对上述四个 Q-递推，存在一个与 aperture m 无关的有限 carry 图，并允许转移规则依赖 mmodhβ​；所有 sufficiently large m 的差分碰撞图长路径均由该固定图控制。

这不是把完全分类重新写成假设。它把指数级、随 m 变化的 fiber-product 图压缩成固定有限图；一旦建立，最终周期性和精确延迟表由有限图计算得出。
最短证明路线
先完成排名 1 的 1101 基数。随后分别处理：


111：递推最简单，有限枚举显示 m≥4 时延迟恒为 2；


101：三步消元应给出按 mmod3 的终端路径分类；


10001：Parry 多项式分解
x5−x4−1=(x2−x+1)(x3−x−1)
暗示必须保留模 6 的边界相位；这是整个分类中唯一真正困难的部分。


最便宜且有决定性的证伪测试
对四个基数分别构造候选固定 carry 图，而不是继续扩大原始的 3m−1 状态图。若 carry 的绝对值不能获得与 m 无关的上界，或新的状态持续随 m 出现，则“最终有限表”路线应立即放弃。
本轮独立有限检查已经给出一个重要警告：对 x3−x−1 的根，aperture m=6,…,11 单射，但 m=12,13,14 又重新非单射，m=15,16 再次单射。因此任何“达到某个阈值后永久单射”的候选都已被否决；只有保留 aperture 相位的最终周期分类仍与数据相容。

三、综合排序依据
排名 1 的优势在于：桥接引理只有一个固定递推、一个二进制差分字母表和奇偶两类终端状态；有限检验已显示极整齐的精确公式。其成功后还会把现稿的“固定代数次数但变化基数”无界性提升为“固定基数、固定字母表”无界性，概念增量最大且证明风险可控。
排名 2 的潜在影响更广，但其成败取决于 10001 基数的非单调碰撞结构能否压缩为固定相位 carry 图。当前数据支持“复杂但有限”，尚不足以支持最终周期性证明。因此其期望值低于排名 1。
第三项不列入。 向一般 simple-Parry 基数、任意 Parry 基数或任意正则数系扩张时，Parry 多项式的互补因子可具有非 cyclotomic 的复根，当前工具尚不能保证 aperture 参数上的 carry 状态稳定；此时既没有参数一致桥接，也没有比原碰撞图指数界更强的可证目标。列入第三项会违反用户规定的“标准对象桥接、非循环性、参数一致性及自身证伪测试”四项合取。

核验边界
我已亲自核验：


现稿关于 rank congruence 非 β-value normalization 的范围声明；


Theorem 6.2 的 Toeplitz／碰撞图精确公式；


Bassino 开放问题和 Charlier–Massuir–Rigo–Rowland Problem 1 的原始定位；


θ 的多项式恒等式、Parry 字 1101、Qm​ 递推；


排名 1 在 2≤m≤16 的完整有限碰撞图；


四个二进制三次 Pisot 多项式的系数界枚举及上述有限 aperture 数据。


尚未核验为定理的是：


排名 1 的参数一致四步 carry 完备分类；


排名 2 的固定 carry 图存在性及最终周期表；


“未发现其他可达命名公开问题”依赖本轮检索所覆盖的文献数据库，不能解释为对全部未数字化文献的逻辑穷尽。


现稿自身亦明确规定计算脚本只作回归检验而非参数一致证明；上述有限新检验同样仅承担证伪筛查，不承担证明。

定理：负共轭二次族的两窗口因果长度
令 a≥b≥1，令
P(z)=z2−az−b,Q0​=1,Q1​=a+1,Qj+2​=aQj+1​+bQj​.

e0​,…,em​∈[−a,a]∩Z
满足
j=0∑m−1​Qj​ej​≡0(modQm​),j=0∑m−1​Qj​ej+1​≡0(modQm​),(1)

证明

L:Z[z]⟶Z,L(j∑​rj​zj)=j∑​rj​Qj​.

L(zjP)=Qj+2​−aQj+1​−bQj​=0(j≥0).(2)

有界边界余式消除
若
R(z)=j≥0∑​rj​zj∈Z[z],∣rj​∣≤a,L(R)=0,(3)
则
P∣R.(4)
事实上，将 R 除以首一多项式 P，可唯一写成
R=PH+u+vz,H∈Z[z].
由 (2) 以及 Q0​=1,Q1​=a+1，
0=L(R)=u+(a+1)v.
故存在 τ∈Z，使

uj​=(−1)j+1hj​.(6)

从而 u1​≥1。

因此归纳得到
uj​≥1(j≥0).(10)
然而 H 为多项式，故充分大的 j 应有 uj​=0，矛盾。于是 τ=0，从而 P∣R。
这里的 (6)–(10) 即所需的参数一致有界进位不变量：任何非零常数边界进位都会强制交错商进位始终为正，因而不可能在有限位置终止。它不依赖枚举，并覆盖商多项式的全部整数分支。

由 (1)，存在整数 κ0​,κ1​ 使
j=0∑m−1​Qj​ej​=κ0​Qm​,j=0∑m−1​Qj​ej+1​=κ1​Qm​.(11)

故

定义
R0​(z)=j=0∑m−1​ej​zj−κ0​zm,R1​(z)=j=0∑m−1​ej+1​zj−κ1​zm.(14)

于是
P∣R0​−zR1​=e0​−(κ0​+em​)zm+κ1​zm+1.(16)
对 (16) 应用 L，得到整数恒等式

若 κ1​=0，则由 (13) 有 ∣κ1​∣=1。现稿的最近倍数分离引理断言，对 m≥4 及
1≤∣e∣≤a，
dist(beQm−1​,Qm​Z)≥Qm−2​.(18)

取 e=κ1​，而 (17) 给出
dist(bκ1​Qm−1​,Qm​Z)≤∣e0​∣≤a.(19)
另一方面，m≥4 且 Qj​ 严格递增，故

与 (18)–(19) 矛盾。因此
κ1​=0.(20)
将其代入 (17)，得到
e0​=(κ0​+em​)Qm​.(21)
由于 ∣e0​∣≤a<Qm​，只能有

这证明了两同余蕴含首坐标消失。

Nβ,m,2​=∅.
另一方面，既有单窗口差块
(b,a,−1,0,…,0)
具有非零首坐标，并满足

故 Nβ,m,1​=∅；这正是现稿已有的单窗口下界机制。 因而
ℓcau​(β,m)=2对全部 a≥b≥1, m≥4.
最近倍数分离界在此并非仅与结论相容，而是精确排除了唯一可能残留的非零末端窗口进位 κ1​=±1；新的交错商进位不变量则排除了此前除差论证遗漏的非零常数余式分支。
